# Canonicalisation report

Generated by `pipeline/resolve.py`.

- canonical settings: 2939 (from 3930 in-scope extracted settings)
- settings outside the ontology's scope: 244; 179 multi-agent / distributed learning; 39 state-dependent reward dynamics (rested / restless Markov arms); 26 state-dependent reward dynamics
- results: 6920; with a registry algorithm: 452
- algorithms: 133 registered, 3437 unregistered mentions (provisional ids)
- citations resolved to corpus papers: 1930/4023; 745 distinct candidate papers outside the corpus
- expressions changed by conversion rules: 109

- `L_g` (2 papers): number of SUW phases (significant winner switches w.r.t. unknown weightings) / constant L with g_i'(a) >= 1/L on the relevant interval (Assumption 3)
- `n_tilde` (2 papers): floor(n/logbar n) * eta_P * log(1/rho) / C_1 / block size ceil(max{C, (16 log(2T/delta))^{1/varepsilon}, (2*4^{2/alpha} log(4/d
- `D_theta` (2 papers): upper bound D of the mean range [0,D] / gradient bound for theta
- `S_beta` (2 papers): number of skipped rounds |S_beta| (rounds with d_t >= beta) / number of rounds with d_t >= beta
- `beta_hat` (2 papers): upper bound on local smoothness constant / upper bound on local smoothness constant beta
- `alpha_v` (2 papers): variance floor parameter alpha / safe constant alpha (sample-size exponent)
- `lambda_p` (2 papers): prior standard deviation lambda of theta* ~ N(0, lambda^2 I), also the algorithm / lambda'(floor(T/H)): smallest real > lambda with N*lambda' integer
- `Gamma_min` (2 papers): minimum information-sharing gain over the final partition / minimum information-sharing gain over the final partition, >= 1
- `m_X` (2 papers): known bound on ||X|| / upper bound on the norm of the node vector, ||X|| <= m
- `alpha_M` (2 papers): largest support point of Z / M/|S|, M = number of randomly picked super arms per round, |S| = number of feasi
- `tau_m` (2 papers): probability that the delay is at most m, P(D <= m) / probability that a delay is at most the window: P(D_1 <= m)
- `eps_sum` (2 papers): sum_t E[eps_t] / cumulative optimization error sum_t eps_t of the optimistic subproblem
- `gamma_s` (2 papers): safety threshold gamma / bound on the second moment of the score function of the exploration distribution
- `d_T` (2 papers): number of rounds the conservative action is played up to T / eluder dimension dim_E(F, l_min/(T*n_max))
- `Omega` (2 papers): problem-dependent constant defined in the theorem (max of two expressions in B,  / sum_k max{c_k^2, (1-c_k)^2}, c_k the fairness requirement of client k
- `w_L` (2 papers): f(a*) - m_L mu_L + mu_1 / smallest mean w(L)
- `b_conv` (2 papers): conversation rate: b(t) = b*t key-term conversations up to round t, b in (0,1) / conversation rate: b(t) = b*t, b in (0,1)
- `lambda_B` (2 papers): minimum eigenvalue of E_{k~unif(B)}[x_tilde_k x_tilde_k^T] for the barycentric s / minimum eigenvalue of E[(x-y)(x-y)^T] for x,y sampled from the key-term barycent
- `C_7` (2 papers): constant depending on eps_m, a, eta: Gamma(1/eps+2)[a/(6+3eta)]^(1/eps)[3/psi(6+ / unspecified constant
- `E_err` (2 papers): total prediction error sum_t ||l_t-m_t||_inf^2 / T * E[||l-m||_inf^2]
- `mu_0` (2 papers): mean reward of default arm / baseline value V^{pi_0}
- `alpha_rucb` (2 papers): RUCB/RLCB scale parameter alpha / RUCB exploration parameter alpha > 1/2
- `L_C` (2 papers): number of losses of a Copeland winner / number of arms that beat a Copeland winner
- `C_abs` (2 papers): unspecified absolute constant / absolute constant independent of T and the sequences
- `L_dens` (2 papers): upper bound L >= 1 on the conditional noise densities / upper bound on conditional noise densities
- `S_tilde` (2 papers): norm of the projected user profile ||w~*|| / ||theta~_*||_2, norm of the key-term-level true parameter
- `alpha_lr` (2 papers): learning-rate constant (eta_t = alpha_lr/sqrt(t)), 0<alpha_lr<1 / learning-rate constant of the algorithm (eta_t = alpha_lr/sqrt(t)), 0<alpha_lr<1
- `B_reg` (2 papers): regret bound B that the algorithm guarantees over H_T(alpha') / upper bound on the algorithm's worst-case regret over H_T(alpha')
